# No-Delete List

Generated: 2026-05-31. During restructuring, preserve every listed route, asset, download, data file, and component unless a later migration explicitly maps it to a replacement and verifies inbound links.

## Preserve Principles

- Do not delete routes without a documented replacement or redirect.
- Do not delete duplicated or suffixed files such as `index 2.html`; mark them for later review first.
- Do not delete downloads, generated data, imported images, scripts, or the separate `woek-akademie-app/` without owner review.
- Treat existing dirty working-tree changes as user-owned.
